pocs/search-celeb PoC(Next.js, 포트 3103) 코드를 읽고 입출력 결함을 찾아라. 범위: 이름 검색(src/server/search.ts, src/lib/names.ts), 사진으로 찾기(src/server/identify.ts, src/components/IdentifyPanel.tsx, src/flow/CelebSearchFlow.tsx), 업로드(pocs/core/src/server/uploads.ts, proxy.ts, next.config.ts), 이미지 수집(src/server/images.ts). 실행 증거: (1) 10MB 넘는 정상 JPEG 업로드 시 400 'Failed to parse body as FormData.' 로그에 'Request body exceeded 10MB ... middlewareClientMaxBodySize'. (2) 40MB 업로드는 413. (3) 45MP PNG는 400 'Input image exceeds pixel limit'. (4) 사진 글자로 후보가 나와도 'SERPAPI_API_KEY가 없어 ... 이름으로 검색해 주세요' 안내가 같이 나옴. (5) 업로드 실패 후에도 IdentifyPanel이 '이름 후보를 찾고 있습니다' 문구로 남음. (6) type=search 입력에서 Esc가 입력값을 지움. 이 증거를 검토하고, 추가로 코드에서 보이는 입출력 결함(경계값, 오류 처리, 얼굴로 신원 판단 금지 정책 위반 가능성 포함)을 파일:줄과 함께 짧게 한국어로 나열해라. 확실한 것만.

     7	import { z } from 'zod';
     8	import type { CelebImage, CelebImageCategory, Issue, UploadRecord } from '@celeber/core/contracts';
     9	import { callVisionStructured } from '@celeber/core/server/ai';
    10	import { celebImages, celebs, newId, nowIso } from '@celeber/core/server/db';
    11	import { getCapabilities } from '@celeber/core/server/env';
    12	import { cacheRemoteImage } from '@celeber/core/server/fetch-image';
    13	import { getImageSearchProviders, searchImagesCached, type ImageSearchProvider, type ImageSearchResult } from '@celeber/core/server/search';
    14	import { toImageRef } from '@celeber/core/server/uploads';
    15	import { judgeByMetadata, rankSearchResults } from '../lib/filter';
    16	import { buildCategoryQueries } from '../lib/queries';
    17	import type { CollectImagesResponse } from '../lib/types';
    18	import { mapLimit, shortWarning } from './util';
    19	
    20	/** 카테고리당 저장 목표 (3열 그리드 3줄) */
    21	export const IMAGES_PER_CATEGORY = 9;
    22	const FETCH = { maxBytes: 10 * 1024 * 1024, timeoutMs: 10_000 };
    23	
    24	const ISSUE_CODES = ['not-full-body', 'multiple-people', 'backlight', 'occluded', 'low-resolution', 'blurry', 'no-person', 'not-a-photo', 'other'] as const;
    25	const JudgeSchema = z.object({
    26	  items: z.array(
    27	    z.object({
    28	      index: z.number().int(),
    29	      isFullBody: z.boolean(),
    30	      analyzable: z.boolean(),
    31	      personCount: z.number().int(),
    32	      issues: z.array(z.object({ code: z.enum(ISSUE_CODES), message: z.string() })),
    33	    }),
    34	  ),
    35	});
    36	type Judge = z.infer<typeof JudgeSchema>;
    37	
    38	const JUDGE_SYSTEM = [
    39	  '연예인 코디 분석용 후보 사진을 선별합니다. 인물이 누구인지는 판단하지 않습니다.',
    40	  '각 이미지에 대해 판정하세요:',
    41	  '- isFullBody: 한 인물의 머리부터 발(또는 신발)까지 대부분 보이면 true.',
    42	  '- analyzable: 상의·하의·신발 등 코디와 피부·헤어 색을 읽을 수 있으면 true. 심한 역광, 큰 가림, 저해상도, 비슷한 크기의 여러 명, 일러스트·합성·콜라주는 false.',
    43	  '- personCount: 보이는 사람 수.',
    44	  '- issues: 문제가 있을 때만, code와 한국어 한 줄 message (10자 내외).',
    45	  'index는 이미지 라벨의 번호와 같아야 합니다. 모든 이미지에 대해 하나씩 답하세요.',
    46	].join('\n');
    47	
    48	export interface Downloaded {
    49	  result: ImageSearchResult & { query: string };
    50	  record: UploadRecord;
    51	  sourceUrl: string;
    52	  thumbnailOnly: boolean;
    53	}
    54	
    55	/**
    56	 * 한 카테고리의 사진을 모은다. 이미 충분하면 저장된 것을 그대로 돌려준다(검색 결과 캐시).
    57	 * refresh=true면 선택하지 않은 사진을 지우고 다시 검색한다.
    58	 */
    59	export async function collectCelebImages(celebId: string, category: CelebImageCategory, opts: { refresh?: boolean; target?: number; now?: Date } = {}): Promise<CollectImagesResponse | null> {
    60	  const celeb = celebs.get(celebId);
    61	  if (!celeb) return null;
    62	  const target = opts.target ?? IMAGES_PER_CATEGORY;
    63	  const notices: string[] = [];
    64	  const queries = buildCategoryQueries(celeb.name, category, opts.now ?? new Date(), celeb.description);
    65	
    66	  if (opts.refresh) celebImages.deleteWhere({ celeb_id: celebId, category, selected: false });
    67	  const existing = listCategory(celebId, category);
    68	  const providers = getImageSearchProviders();
    69	  const base = { celebId, category, queries, provider: providers[0].id } as const;
    70	  if (existing.length >= target) {
    71	    return { ...base, images: existing, added: 0, failed: 0, judge: { origin: existing.some((i) => i.reasons.some((r) => r.code.startsWith('ai-'))) ? 'ai' : 'rules' }, isMock: existing.some((i) => i.isMock), notices };
    72	  }
    73	
    74	  // 1) 검색 (프로바이더 실패 시 다음 프로바이더)
    75	  const { results, provider, errors } = await searchAll(providers, queries);
    76	  notices.push(...errors);
    77	  const existingUrls = new Set(listAllUrls(celebId));
    78	  const ranked = rankSearchResults(results, existingUrls);
    79	  const need = target - existing.length;
    80	
    81	  // 2) 서버 캐시 (외부 핫링크 대신 /api/uploads/<id>)
    82	  const downloaded: Downloaded[] = [];
    83	  let failed = 0;
    84	  let cursor = 0;
    85	  const maxAttempts = need + 10;
    86	  while (downloaded.length < need && cursor < ranked.length && cursor < maxAttempts) {
    87	    const batch = ranked.slice(cursor, cursor + Math.min(4, need - downloaded.length + 1));
    88	    cursor += batch.length;
    89	    const got = await mapLimit(batch, 4, (r) => download(r, provider, celeb.name, category));
    90	    for (const g of got) {
    91	      if (g && downloaded.length < need && !existingUrls.has(g.sourceUrl)) {
    92	        downloaded.push(g);
    93	        existingUrls.add(g.sourceUrl);
    94	      } else if (!g) failed++;
    95	    }
    96	  }
    97	  if (failed) notices.push(`${failed}장은 원본 서버에서 내려받지 못해 제외했습니다.`);
    98	  if (!ranked.length) notices.push('검색 결과가 없습니다. 이름 표기를 바꿔 다시 찾아보세요.');
    99	
   100	  // 3) 전신/분석 가능 판정
   101	  const judged = await judgeImages(downloaded, provider.id === 'mock');
   102	  if (judged.warning && judged.origin !== 'ai' && provider.id !== 'mock') notices.push('AI 판정을 사용할 수 없어 사진 크기로만 전신 여부를 추정했습니다.');
   103	
   104	  // 4) 저장 (전신 추정 먼저)
   105	  const now = nowIso();
   106	  const order = downloaded.map((d, i) => ({ d, j: judged.items[i] })).sort((a, b) => rankOf(a.j) - rankOf(b.j));
   107	  for (const { d, j } of order) {
   108	    const image: CelebImage = {
   109	      id: newId('cimg'),
   110	      celebId,
   111	      createdAt: now,
   112	      category,
   113	      query: d.result.query,
   114	      provider: provider.id,
   115	      sourceUrl: d.sourceUrl,
   116	      pageUrl: d.result.pageUrl,
   117	      sourceTitle: [d.result.title, d.result.source].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') || undefined,
   118	      cached: toImageRef(d.record),
   119	      thumbnailUrl: d.result.thumbnailUrl?.startsWith('http') ? d.result.thumbnailUrl : undefined,
   120	      width: d.record.width ?? d.result.width,
   121	      height: d.record.height ?? d.result.height,
   122	      isFullBody: j.isFullBody,
   123	      analyzable: j.analyzable,
   124	      reasons: j.reasons,
   125	      selected: false,
   126	      isMock: provider.id === 'mock',
   127	    };
   128	    try {
   129	      celebImages.insert({
   130	        id: image.id,
   131	        data: image,
   132	        cols: {
   133	          celeb_id: celebId,
   134	          category,
   135	          provider: provider.id,
   136	          source_url: image.sourceUrl,
   137	          upload_id: d.record.id,
   138	          is_full_body: image.isFullBody,
   139	          analyzable: image.analyzable,
   140	          selected: false,
   141	          is_mock: image.isMock,
   142	        },
   143	      });
   144	    } catch (e) {
   145	      // UNIQUE(celeb_id, source_url): 동시에 같은 카테고리를 수집한 경우
   146	      if (!/UNIQUE/i.test((e as Error).message)) throw e;
   147	    }
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  // 인물 썸네일이 없으면 첫 전신 사진으로
   151	  const images = listCategory(celebId, category);
   152	  if (!celeb.thumbnail) {
   153	    const first = images.find((i) => i.isFullBody !== false && i.cached);
   154	    if (first?.cached) {
   155	      celebs.update(celebId, { data: { ...celeb, thumbnail: first.cached, updatedAt: nowIso() }, cols: { thumbnail_upload_id: first.cached.uploadId } });
   156	    }
   157	  }
   158	  if (provider.id === 'mock') notices.push('이미지 검색 키가 없어 데모 이미지를 보여줍니다. 실루엣은 분석할 수 없고, 데모 사진은 검색한 인물과 무관한 데모 모델입니다.');
   159	
   160	  return {
   161	    ...base,
   162	    provider: provider.id,
   163	    images,
   164	    added: downloaded.length,
   165	    failed,
   166	    judge: { origin: judged.origin, warning: shortWarning(judged.warning) },
   167	    isMock: provider.id === 'mock',
   168	    notices,
   169	  };
   170	}
   171	
   172	function listCategory(celebId: string, category: CelebImageCategory): CelebImage[] {
   173	  return celebImages.list({ where: { celeb_id: celebId, category }, order: 'created_at ASC', limit: 100 }).map((r) => r.data);
   174	}
   175	function listAllUrls(celebId: string): string[] {
   176	  return celebImages.list({ where: { celeb_id: celebId }, limit: 500 }).map((r) => r.data.sourceUrl);
   177	}
   178	
   179	const rankOf = (j: { isFullBody: boolean | null; analyzable: boolean | null }) => (j.isFullBody === true ? (j.analyzable === false ? 1 : 0) : j.isFullBody === null ? 2 : 3);
   180	
   181	async function searchAll(providers: ImageSearchProvider[], queries: string[]): Promise<{ results: (ImageSearchResult & { query: string })[]; provider: ImageSearchProvider; errors: string[] }> {
   182	  const errors: string[] = [];
   183	  for (const provider of providers) {
   184	    const results: (ImageSearchResult & { query: string })[] = [];
   185	    let ok = 0;
   186	    for (const query of queries) {
   187	      try {
   188	        const res = await searchImagesCached({ query, count: provider.id === 'mock' ? 6 : 20, large: true }, { provider });
   189	        results.push(...res.map((r) => ({ ...r, query })));
   190	        ok++;
   191	      } catch (e) {
   192	        errors.push(`${provider.id} 검색 실패: ${(e as Error).message}`);
   193	      }
   194	    }
   195	    if (ok > 0) return { results, provider, errors };
   196	  }
   197	  return { results: [], provider: providers[providers.length - 1], errors };
   198	}
   199	
   200	/** 원본 → 실패 시 썸네일로 캐시. 성공하면 Downloaded */
   201	async function download(r: ImageSearchResult & { query: string }, provider: ImageSearchProvider, name: string, category: CelebImageCategory): Promise<Downloaded | null> {
   202	  const sourceUrl = r.imageUrl.startsWith('data:') ? `mock://celeb/${encodeURIComponent(name)}/${category}/${crypto.createHash('sha1').update(r.imageUrl).digest('hex').slice(0, 16)}` : r.imageUrl;
   203	  try {
   204	    const record = await cacheRemoteImage(r.imageUrl, { fetch: FETCH });
   205	    return { result: r, record, sourceUrl, thumbnailOnly: false };
   206	  } catch (e) {
   207	    if (r.thumbnailUrl && r.thumbnailUrl !== r.imageUrl && provider.id !== 'mock') {
   208	      try {
   209	        const record = await cacheRemoteImage(r.thumbnailUrl, { fetch: FETCH });
   210	        return { result: r, record, sourceUrl, thumbnailOnly: true };
   211	      } catch {
   212	        /* 둘 다 실패 */
   213	      }
   214	    }
   215	    console.warn('[search-celeb] 이미지 캐시 실패:', (e as Error).message);
   216	    return null;
   217	  }
   218	}
   219	
   220	interface JudgeOut {
   221	  items: { isFullBody: boolean | null; analyzable: boolean | null; reasons: Issue[] }[];
   222	  origin: 'ai' | 'rules' | 'mock';
   223	  warning?: string;
   224	}
   225	
   226	function metaJudge(d: Downloaded): JudgeOut['items'][number] {
   227	  const size = { width: d.record.width ?? d.result.width, height: d.record.height ?? d.result.height };
   228	  const j = judgeByMetadata(size);
   229	  const reasons = [...j.reasons];
   230	  if (d.thumbnailOnly) reasons.push({ code: 'thumbnail-only', message: '원본을 받지 못해 작은 미리보기만 저장했습니다.', severity: 'warn' });
   231	  // 데모 실루엣(SVG)은 사람 검출이 되지 않으므로 미리 '분석 불가'로 표시한다. 래스터 데모 사진은 분석 가능.
   232	  const demoSilhouette = d.result.provider === 'mock' && d.result.imageUrl.startsWith('data:image/svg');
   233	  if (demoSilhouette) reasons.push({ code: 'demo', message: '데모 실루엣 · 분석 불가 (인물 사진이 아님)', severity: 'warn' });
   234	  else if (d.result.provider === 'mock') reasons.push({ code: 'demo', message: '데모 사진 (검색한 인물과 무관한 데모 모델)', severity: 'info' });
   235	  return { isFullBody: j.isFullBody, analyzable: d.thumbnailOnly || demoSilhouette ? false : j.analyzable, reasons };
   236	}
   237	
   238	/** 전신·분석 가능 판정 (키 있으면 AI, 없으면 메타데이터). export는 실제 AI 확인 테스트용 */
   239	export async function judgeImages(list: Downloaded[], isMockProvider: boolean): Promise<JudgeOut> {
   240	  const meta = list.map(metaJudge);
   241	  if (!list.length || isMockProvider) return { items: meta, origin: isMockProvider ? 'mock' : 'rules' };
   242	  if (!getCapabilities().ai) return { items: meta, origin: 'rules', warning: 'AI 사용 불가' };
   243	  const r = await callVisionStructured<Judge>({
   244	    schema: JudgeSchema,
   245	    mock: () => ({ items: [] }),
   246	    system: JUDGE_SYSTEM,
   247	    prompt: `이미지 ${list.length}장을 판정해 주세요. index는 1부터 ${list.length}까지입니다.`,
   248	    images: list.map((d, i) => ({ uploadId: d.record.id, label: `이미지 ${i + 1}` })),
   249	    maxImageEdge: 512,
   250	    maxTokens: 6000,
   251	    effort: 'low',
   252	    label: 'search-celeb/judge-full-body',
   253	  });
   254	  if (r.source !== 'ai') return { items: meta, origin: 'rules', warning: r.warning };
   255	  const byIndex = new Map(r.data.items.map((it) => [it.index, it]));
   256	  return {
   257	    origin: 'ai',
   258	    items: meta.map((m, i) => {
   259	      const a = byIndex.get(i + 1);
   260	      if (!a) return m;
   261	      const reasons: Issue[] = [
   262	        { code: 'ai-judged', message: `AI 판정 · 인물 ${a.personCount}명`, severity: 'info' },
   263	        ...a.issues.map((x) => ({ code: x.code, message: x.message, severity: 'warn' as const })),
   264	        ...m.reasons.filter((x) => x.code === 'low-resolution' || x.code === 'thumbnail-only'),
   265	      ];
   266	      return { isFullBody: a.isFullBody, analyzable: a.analyzable && m.analyzable !== false, reasons };
   267	    }),
   268	  };
   269	}

     1	/**
     2	 * 업로드/캐시 이미지 저장과 서빙. 파일: <dataDir>/uploads/<id>.<ext>, 메타: uploads 테이블.

    18	export const MAX_STORED_EDGE = 2560;

    20	const ID_RE = /^up_[a-z0-9]+$/;

    22	  'image/jpeg': 'jpg',
    23	  'image/png': 'png',

    36	export const uploadUrl = (id: string): string => `/api/uploads/${id}`;

    38	/** 매직 바이트로 이미지 MIME 판별 (확장자/헤더 신뢰 안 함) */
    39	export function sniffImageMime(buf: Buffer): string | null {
    40	  if (buf.length < 12) return null;
    41	  if (buf[0] === 0xff && buf[1] === 0xd8 && buf[2] === 0xff) return 'image/jpeg';
    42	  if (buf[0] === 0x89 && buf[1] === 0x50 && buf[2] === 0x4e && buf[3] === 0x47) return 'image/png';
    43	  if (buf.toString('ascii', 0, 4) === 'RIFF' && buf.toString('ascii', 8, 12) === 'WEBP') return 'image/webp';
    44	  if (buf.toString('ascii', 0, 4) === 'GIF8') return 'image/gif';
    45	  const ftyp = buf.toString('ascii', 4, 12);
    46	  if (ftyp.startsWith('ftyp')) {
    47	    const brand = ftyp.slice(4);
    48	    if (['heic', 'heix', 'mif1', 'msf1', 'hevc'].includes(brand)) return 'image/heic';
    49	    if (['avif', 'avis'].includes(brand)) return 'image/avif';
    50	  }
    51	  const head = buf.toString('utf8', 0, Math.min(buf.length, 512)).trimStart();
    52	  if (head.startsWith('<svg') || (head.startsWith('<?xml') && head.includes('<svg'))) return 'image/svg+xml';
    53	  return null;
    54	}

    56	interface UploadRow {
    57	  id: string;
    58	  created_at: string;
    59	  kind: string;
    60	  mime: string;
    61	  bytes: number;
    62	  width: number | null;
    63	  height: number | null;
    64	  sha256: string;
    65	  source_url: string | null;
    66	  file_name: string;
    67	}
    68	const toRecord = (r: UploadRow): UploadRecord => ({
    69	  id: r.id,
    70	  createdAt: r.created_at,
    71	  kind: UploadKindSchema.catch('other').parse(r.kind),
    72	  mime: r.mime,
    73	  bytes: Number(r.bytes),
    74	  width: r.width === null ? null : Number(r.width),
    75	  height: r.height === null ? null : Number(r.height),
    76	  sha256: r.sha256,
    77	  sourceUrl: r.source_url,
    78	  url: uploadUrl(r.id),
    79	});

    81	export interface SaveUploadOptions {

    86	  normalize?: boolean;

    91	/** 이미지 버퍼 저장 → UploadRecord. 이미지가 아니면 예외. */
    92	export async function saveUpload(input: Buffer | Uint8Array | ArrayBuffer, opts: SaveUploadOptions): Promise<UploadRecord> {
    93	  let buf: Buffer = Buffer.isBuffer(input) ? input : Buffer.from(input instanceof ArrayBuffer ? new Uint8Array(input) : input);
    94	  if (buf.length > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) throw new Error(`이미지가 너무 큽니다 (최대 ${MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES / 1024 / 1024}MB).`);
    95	  let mime = sniffImageMime(buf);
    96	  if (!mime) throw new Error('지원하지 않는 이미지 형식입니다 (JPEG/PNG/WebP/HEIC/AVIF/GIF/SVG).');
    97	  let width: number | null = null;
    98	  let height: number | null = null;
    99	
   100	  if (mime !== 'image/svg+xml') {
   101	    const normalize = opts.normalize ?? true;
   102	    const img = sharp(buf, { failOn: 'none', limitInputPixels: MAX_INPUT_PIXELS });
   103	    if (normalize) {
   104	      const maxEdge = opts.maxEdge ?? MAX_STORED_EDGE;
   105	      const outPng = mime === 'image/png' || mime === 'image/gif';
   106	      const pipeline = img.rotate().resize({ width: maxEdge, height: maxEdge, fit: 'inside', withoutEnlargement: true }).toColorspace('srgb');
   107	      const { data, info } = await (outPng ? pipeline.png() : pipeline.jpeg({ quality: 92, chromaSubsampling: '4:4:4' })).toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true });
   108	      buf = data;
   109	      mime = outPng ? 'image/png' : 'image/jpeg';
   110	      width = info.width;
   111	      height = info.height;
   112	    } else {
   113	      const meta = await img.metadata();
   114	      width = meta.width ?? null;
   115	      height = meta.height ?? null;
   116	    }
   117	  }
   118	
   119	  const sha256 = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buf).digest('hex');
   120	  const db = getDb();
   121	  const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM uploads WHERE sha256 = ? AND kind = ? LIMIT 1').get(sha256, opts.kind) as UploadRow | undefined;
   122	  if (existing && fs.existsSync(path.join(uploadsDir(), existing.file_name))) return toRecord(existing);
   123	
   124	  const id = newId('up');
   125	  const fileName = `${id}.${EXT[mime] ?? 'bin'}`;
   126	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(uploadsDir(), fileName), buf);
   127	  const row: UploadRow = {
   128	    id,
   129	    created_at: nowIso(),
   130	    kind: opts.kind,
   131	    mime,
   132	    bytes: buf.length,
   133	    width,
   134	    height,
   135	    sha256,
   136	    source_url: opts.sourceUrl ?? null,
   137	    file_name: fileName,
   138	  };
   139	  db.prepare(
   140	    'INSERT INTO uploads (id, created_at, kind, mime, bytes, width, height, sha256, source_url, file_name) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
   141	  ).run(row.id, row.created_at, row.kind, row.mime, row.bytes, row.width, row.height, row.sha256, row.source_url, row.file_name);
   142	  return toRecord(row);
   143	}
   144	
   145	/** data URL(base64) 저장 — 클라이언트 canvas.toDataURL() 결과 */
   146	export async function saveUploadFromDataUrl(dataUrl: string, opts: SaveUploadOptions): Promise<UploadRecord> {
   147	  const m = /^data:([\w/+.-]+);base64,(.*)$/s.exec(dataUrl);
   148	  if (m) return saveUpload(Buffer.from(m[2], 'base64'), opts);
   149	  const svg = /^data:image\/svg\+xml(?:;charset=[\w-]+)?,(.*)$/s.exec(dataUrl);
   150	  if (svg) return saveUpload(Buffer.from(decodeURIComponent(svg[1]), 'utf8'), opts);
   151	  throw new Error('data URL 형식이 아닙니다.');
   152	}
   153	
   154	export function getUpload(id: string): UploadRecord | null {
   155	  if (!ID_RE.test(id)) return null;
   156	  const r = getDb().prepare('SELECT * FROM uploads WHERE id = ?').get(id) as UploadRow | undefined;
   157	  return r ? toRecord(r) : null;
   158	}
   159	/** 같은 원본 URL의 캐시 사본 */
   160	export function findUploadBySourceUrl(sourceUrl: string): UploadRecord | null {
   161	  const r = getDb().prepare('SELECT * FROM uploads WHERE source_url = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1').get(sourceUrl) as UploadRow | undefined;
   162	  return r ? toRecord(r) : null;
   163	}
   164	
   165	export function readUpload(id: string): { record: UploadRecord; buffer: Buffer } | null {
   166	  if (!ID_RE.test(id)) return null;
   167	  const r = getDb().prepare('SELECT * FROM uploads WHERE id = ?').get(id) as UploadRow | undefined;
   168	  if (!r) return null;
   169	  const p = path.join(uploadsDir(), r.file_name);
   170	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) return null;
   171	  return { record: toRecord(r), buffer: fs.readFileSync(p) };
   172	}
   173	
   174	export function deleteUpload(id: string): boolean {
   175	  if (!ID_RE.test(id)) return false;
   176	  const r = getDb().prepare('SELECT file_name FROM uploads WHERE id = ?').get(id) as { file_name: string } | undefined;
   177	  if (!r) return false;
   178	  fs.rmSync(path.join(uploadsDir(), r.file_name), { force: true });
   179	  getDb().prepare('DELETE FROM uploads WHERE id = ?').run(id);
   180	  return true;
   181	}

   183	/** UploadRecord → 계약 ImageRef */
   184	export function toImageRef(r: UploadRecord): ImageRef {
   185	  return {
   186	    uploadId: r.id,
   187	    url: r.url,
   188	    ...(r.width ? { width: r.width } : {}),
   189	    ...(r.height ? { height: r.height } : {}),
   190	    ...(r.sourceUrl ? { sourceUrl: r.sourceUrl } : {}),
   191	  };
   192	}
   193	
   194	/** GET /api/uploads/[id] 구현 */
   195	export function serveUpload(id: string, req?: Request): Response {
   196	  const f = readUpload(id);
   197	  if (!f) return jsonError(404, 'not_found', '이미지를 찾을 수 없습니다.');
   198	  const etag = `"${f.record.sha256.slice(0, 32)}"`;
   199	  if (req?.headers.get('if-none-match') === etag) return new Response(null, { status: 304, headers: { ETag: etag } });
   200	  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
   201	    'Content-Type': f.record.mime,
   202	    'Content-Length': String(f.buffer.length),
   203	    'Cache-Control': 'private, max-age=31536000, immutable',
   204	    ETag: etag,
   205	    'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff',
   206	  };
   207	  // SVG 내 스크립트 실행 방지
   208	  if (f.record.mime === 'image/svg+xml') headers['Content-Security-Policy'] = "default-src 'none'; style-src 'unsafe-inline'; sandbox";
   209	  return new Response(new Uint8Array(f.buffer), { status: 200, headers });
   210	}
   211	
   212	/**
   213	 * POST /api/uploads 구현: multipart/form-data { file: File, kind?: UploadKind }
   214	 * 또는 JSON { dataUrl: string, kind?: UploadKind } → UploadRecord JSON
   215	 */
   216	/** 업로드 요청 본문 상한: 25MB 이미지의 base64(×4/3) + 여유 */
   217	export const MAX_UPLOAD_BODY_BYTES = Math.ceil(MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES * 1.4) + 1024 * 1024;
   218	/** sharp 디코드 픽셀 상한 (약 40MP). 작은 PNG가 16000×16000을 선언해 1GB를 디코드하는 것을 막는다 */
   219	export const MAX_INPUT_PIXELS = 40_000_000;
   220	
   221	class BodyTooLargeError extends Error {}
   222	
   223	/** 본문을 상한까지만 읽는다. Content-Length가 크면 읽기 전에, 스트림이 상한을 넘으면 그 즉시 중단 */
   224	export async function readBodyCapped(req: Request, max = MAX_UPLOAD_BODY_BYTES): Promise<Uint8Array> {
   225	  const declared = Number(req.headers.get('content-length') ?? '');
   226	  if (Number.isFinite(declared) && declared > max) throw new BodyTooLargeError();
   227	  if (!req.body) return new Uint8Array(0);
   228	  const reader = req.body.getReader();
   229	  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
   230	  let total = 0;
   231	  for (;;) {
   232	    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
   233	    if (done) break;
   234	    total += value.byteLength;
   235	    if (total > max) {
   236	      await reader.cancel().catch(() => {});
   237	      throw new BodyTooLargeError();
   238	    }
   239	    chunks.push(value);
   240	  }
   241	  const out = new Uint8Array(total);
   242	  let o = 0;
   243	  for (const c of chunks) {
   244	    out.set(c, o);
   245	    o += c.byteLength;
   246	  }
   247	  return out;
   248	}
   249	
   250	export async function handleUploadPost(req: Request, defaultKind: UploadKind = 'other'): Promise<Response> {
   251	  try {
   252	    const ct = req.headers.get('content-type') ?? '';
   253	    let bytes: Uint8Array;
   254	    try {
   255	      bytes = await readBodyCapped(req);
   256	    } catch (e) {
   257	      if (e instanceof BodyTooLargeError) return jsonError(413, 'too_large', '이미지가 너무 큽니다 (최대 25MB).');
   258	      throw e;
   259	    }
   260	    if (ct.includes('application/json')) {
   261	      const body = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) as { dataUrl?: string; kind?: string };
   262	      if (!body.dataUrl) return jsonError(400, 'bad_request', 'dataUrl이 필요합니다.');
   263	      const kind = UploadKindSchema.catch(defaultKind).parse(body.kind ?? defaultKind);
   264	      return Response.json(await saveUploadFromDataUrl(body.dataUrl, { kind }));
   265	    }
   266	    const form = await new Response(bytes as BodyInit, { headers: { 'content-type': ct } }).formData();
   267	    const file = form.get('file');
   268	    if (!(file instanceof Blob)) return jsonError(400, 'bad_request', "'file' 필드가 필요합니다.");
   269	    if (file.size > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES) return jsonError(413, 'too_large', '이미지가 너무 큽니다.');
   270	    const kind = UploadKindSchema.catch(defaultKind).parse(String(form.get('kind') ?? defaultKind));
   271	    const rec = await saveUpload(Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer()), { kind });
   272	    return Response.json(rec);
   273	  } catch (e) {
   274	    return jsonError(400, 'upload_failed', (e as Error).message);
   275	  }
   276	}
   277	
   278	/**
   279	 * 분석용 픽셀 읽기 (서버): RGBA raw + 크기. maxEdge로 다운스케일.
   280	 * core/face sampleFaceColors 등 ImageDataLike 입력으로 바로 사용 가능.
   281	 */
   282	export async function readUploadPixels(id: string, maxEdge = 1024): Promise<{ data: Uint8ClampedArray; width: number; height: number } | null> {
   283	  const f = readUpload(id);
   284	  if (!f) return null;
   285	  const { data, info } = await sharp(f.buffer, { limitInputPixels: MAX_INPUT_PIXELS })
   286	    .rotate()
   287	    .resize({ width: maxEdge, height: maxEdge, fit: 'inside', withoutEnlargement: true })
   288	    .toColorspace('srgb')
   289	    .ensureAlpha()
   290	    .raw()
   291	    .toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true });
   292	  return { data: new Uint8ClampedArray(data.buffer, data.byteOffset, data.length), width: info.width, height: info.height };
   293	}

     6	// 워크스페이스 루트(pnpm-workspace.yaml 위치)를 찾아 루트 .env를 로드한다.
     7	function findWorkspaceRoot(start: string): string {
     8	  let dir = start;
     9	  while (dir !== path.dirname(dir)) {
    10	    if (fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, 'pnpm-workspace.yaml'))) return dir;
    11	    dir = path.dirname(dir);
    12	  }
    13	  return path.resolve(start, '../..');
    14	}

    53	export async function identifyFromPhoto(uploadId: string): Promise<IdentifyResponse | null> {
    54	  const rec = getUpload(uploadId);
    55	  if (!rec) return null;
    56	  const caps = getCapabilities();
    57	  const notices: string[] = [];
    58	
    59	  const [reverse, photo] = await Promise.all([
    60	    reverseImageSearch({ uploadId }),
    61	    callVisionStructured<PhotoText>({
    62	      schema: PhotoTextSchema,
    63	      mock: () => ({ visibleTexts: [], nameTexts: [], personCount: -1, framing: 'unknown' as const, note: '' }),
    64	      system: PHOTO_TEXT_SYSTEM,
    65	      prompt: '이 사진에서 글자로 보이는 정보와 구도를 정리해 주세요.',
    66	      images: [{ uploadId, label: '사용자가 올린 연예인 사진' }],
    67	      maxTokens: 4000,
    68	      effort: 'low',
    69	      label: 'search-celeb/photo-text',
    70	    }),
    71	  ]);
    72	
    73	  // 1) 역이미지 검색 텍스트
    74	  let reverseCands: CelebCandidate[] = [];
    75	  if (reverse.status === 'ok') {
    76	    reverseCands = extractNameCandidates(reverse);
    77	    const titles = reverse.visualMatches.map((m) => m.title).slice(0, 30);
    78	    if (titles.length && caps.ai) {
    79	      const r = await callTextStructured<TitleNames>({
    80	        schema: TitleNamesSchema,
    81	        mock: () => ({ names: [] }),
    82	        system: TITLE_SYSTEM,
    83	        prompt: `페이지 제목 ${titles.length}건:\n${titles.map((t, i) => `${i + 1}. ${t}`).join('\n')}`,
    84	        maxTokens: 3000,
    85	        effort: 'low',
    86	        label: 'search-celeb/title-names',
    87	      });
    88	      if (r.source === 'ai') {
    89	        const aiCands: CelebCandidate[] = r.data.names
    90	          .filter((n) => n.name.trim() && n.mentions > 0)
    91	          .map((n) => ({
    92	            name: n.name.trim(),
    93	            nameEn: n.nameEn.trim() || undefined,
    94	            description: n.description.trim() || undefined,
    95	            source: 'reverse-image',
    96	            score: Math.round(Math.min(0.9, n.mentions / titles.length + 0.2) * 100) / 100,
    97	            evidence: [`비슷한 이미지가 실린 페이지 제목 ${titles.length}건 중 ${n.mentions}건에 적힌 이름 (제목 텍스트 정리)`],
    98	          }));
    99	        reverseCands = mergeCandidates([aiCands, reverseCands], 8);
   100	      }
   101	    }
   102	    if (!reverseCands.length) notices.push('역이미지 검색 결과 제목에서 인물 이름을 찾지 못했습니다.');
   103	  } else if (reverse.reason) {
   104	    notices.push(reverse.reason);
   105	  }
   106	
   107	  // 2) 사진 속 글자
   108	  const textCands = photo.source === 'ai' ? photoTextCandidates(photo.data.nameTexts) : [];
   109	
   110	  let candidates = mergeCandidates([reverseCands, textCands], 6);
   111	  let isMock = reverse.provider === 'mock' && photo.source === 'mock';
   112	  if (!candidates.length && (reverse.status !== 'ok' || reverse.provider === 'mock')) {
   113	    // 키 없음: 데모 후보 (흐름 확인용) + 이름 검색 안내
   114	    candidates = MOCK_PEOPLE.map((p) => ({ ...p, evidence: ['데모 후보: 사진 검색 키가 없어 가상 인물을 보여줍니다. 실제 인물은 이름으로 검색해 주세요.'] }));
   115	    isMock = true;
   116	  }
   117	
   118	  const withThumbs = await mapLimit(attachCelebIds(candidates), 3, async (c) => ({
   119	    ...c,
   120	    thumbnailUrl: (await proxyImageUrl(c.thumbnailUrl)) ?? (await candidateThumbnail(c.name, c.description)),
   121	  }));
   122	
   123	  const realCount = withThumbs.filter((c) => c.source !== 'mock').length;
   124	  const needsNameSearch = realCount === 0;
   125	  // 역이미지 검색이 불가하면 그 사유에 이미 이름 검색 안내가 들어 있다
   126	  if (needsNameSearch && reverse.status === 'ok') notices.push('사진만으로는 후보를 찾지 못했습니다. 이름으로 검색해 주세요.');
   127	  if (needsNameSearch && reverse.status !== 'ok' && !notices.some((n) => n.includes('이름으로 검색'))) notices.push('이름으로 검색해 주세요.');
   128	  if (photo.warning && photo.source === 'mock') notices.push('사진 속 글자 읽기를 건너뛰었습니다 (AI 사용 불가).');
   129	
   130	  const framing: PhotoFraming = photo.source === 'ai' ? photo.data.framing : 'unknown';
   131	  return {
   132	    upload: toImageRef(rec),
   133	    reverse: {
   134	      provider: reverse.provider,
   135	      status: reverse.status,
   136	      reason: reverse.reason,
   137	      knowledgeGraphCount: reverse.knowledgeGraph.length,
   138	      visualMatchCount: reverse.visualMatches.length,
   139	    },
   140	    photoText: { texts: photo.source === 'ai' ? photo.data.visibleTexts.slice(0, 15) : [], origin: photo.source === 'ai' ? 'ai' : 'mock', warning: shortWarning(photo.warning) },
   141	    photoCheck: {
   142	      framing,
   143	      personCount: photo.source === 'ai' && photo.data.personCount >= 0 ? photo.data.personCount : null,
   144	      note: photo.source === 'ai' && photo.data.note ? photo.data.note : framingNoteByMeta(rec.width, rec.height),
   145	      origin: photo.source === 'ai' ? 'ai' : 'rules',
   146	    },
   147	    candidates: withThumbs,
   148	    needsNameSearch,
   149	    isMock,
   150	    notices: [...new Set(notices)],
   151	  };
   152	}
   153	
   154	function framingNoteByMeta(w: number | null, h: number | null): string {
   155	  if (!w || !h) return '사진 구도를 판정하지 못했습니다.';
   156	  const a = h / w;
   157	  return a >= 1.25
   158	    ? `세로 사진입니다 (비율 ${a.toFixed(2)}). 전신이 보이면 이 사진으로 바로 분석할 수 있습니다.`
   159	    : `가로 또는 정사각형 사진입니다 (비율 ${a.toFixed(2)}). 전신 분석에는 대표·최근·평소 사진에서 고르는 편이 정확합니다.`;
   160	}

   123	function toCandidates(data: NameCandidates, query: string): CelebCandidate[] {

     1	/**
     2	 * 이름 정규화와 후보 병합. 서버/클라이언트 공용.
     3	 * normalizeNameKey는 core/server/db의 같은 이름 함수와 동일 규칙(서버 전용 모듈이라 여기 복제).
     4	 */
     5	import type { CelebCandidate } from '@celeber/core/contracts';
     6	
     7	/** 이름 조회 키: NFC, 소문자, 공백·구두점 제거 */
     8	export function nameKey(name: string): string {
     9	  return name.normalize('NFC').toLowerCase().replace(/[\s·.\-_()[\]]/g, '');
    10	}
    11	
    12	const SOURCE_RANK: Record<CelebCandidate['source'], number> = {
    13	  'name-search': 0,
    14	  'reverse-image': 1,
    15	  'photo-text': 2,
    16	  mock: 3,
    17	};
    18	
    19	/**
    20	 * 여러 출처의 후보 목록을 이름 키로 합친다. 한쪽의 영문명(nameEn)이 다른 쪽 이름과 같아도 같은 후보로 본다
    21	 * (예: 'Jennie'로 검색하면 입력 이름 'Jennie'와 AI 후보 '제니'(nameEn Jennie)를 하나로).
    22	 * - 같은 이름이 여러 출처에서 나오면 점수에 출처당 +0.1 (최대 1)
    23	 * - 근거(evidence)는 합치고 중복 제거
    24	 * - 설명/영문명/썸네일/링크는 먼저 나온 값 유지
    25	 * 점수는 텍스트 언급 빈도 기반이며 얼굴 유사도가 아니다.
    26	 */
    27	export function mergeCandidates(lists: CelebCandidate[][], limit = 8): CelebCandidate[] {
    28	  const map = new Map<string, { c: CelebCandidate; sources: Set<string>; order: number }>();
    29	  /** 영문명 키 → 그 후보의 이름 키 */
    30	  const enAlias = new Map<string, string>();
    31	  let order = 0;
    32	  for (const list of lists) {
    33	    for (const c of list) {
    34	      const key = nameKey(c.name);
    35	      if (!key) continue;
    36	      const enKey = c.nameEn ? nameKey(c.nameEn) : '';
    37	      const hit = map.get(key) ?? map.get(enAlias.get(key) ?? '') ?? (enKey ? map.get(enKey) : undefined);
    38	      if (!hit) {
    39	        map.set(key, { c: { ...c, evidence: [...c.evidence] }, sources: new Set([c.source]), order: order++ });
    40	        if (enKey && !enAlias.has(enKey)) enAlias.set(enKey, key);
    41	        continue;
    42	      }
    43	      const m = hit.c;
    44	      hit.sources.add(c.source);
    45	      m.nameEn ??= c.nameEn;
    46	      m.description ??= c.description;
    47	      m.thumbnailUrl ??= c.thumbnailUrl;
    48	      m.externalUrl ??= c.externalUrl;
    49	      m.score = Math.max(m.score ?? 0, c.score ?? 0);
    50	      for (const e of c.evidence) if (!m.evidence.includes(e)) m.evidence.push(e);
    51	      if (SOURCE_RANK[c.source] < SOURCE_RANK[m.source]) m.source = c.source;
    52	    }
    53	  }
    54	  return [...map.values()]
    55	    .map(({ c, sources, order: o }) => ({ c: { ...c, score: round2(Math.min(1, (c.score ?? 0.5) + 0.1 * (sources.size - 1))) }, o }))
    56	    .sort((a, b) => (b.c.score ?? 0) - (a.c.score ?? 0) || a.o - b.o)
    57	    .slice(0, limit)
    58	    .map((x) => x.c);
    59	}
    60	
    61	const round2 = (v: number) => Math.round(v * 100) / 100;

    14	import { CelebGallery } from '../components/CelebGallery';
    15	import { CelebProfileHeader } from '../components/CelebProfileHeader';
    16	import { CelebSearchBox } from '../components/CelebSearchBox';
    17	import { IdentifyPanel } from '../components/IdentifyPanel';
    18	import { SavedCelebList } from '../components/SavedCelebList';
    19	import s from '../components/search-celeb.module.css';
    20	
    21	export interface CelebSearchFlowProps {
    22	  apiBase?: string;
    23	  /** 사진 선택 방식 (기본 multiple). celeb-vibe-report처럼 한 장만 필요하면 'single' */
    24	  selection?: 'multiple' | 'single';
    25	  /** 처음부터 열 인물 (URL ?celeb= 등) */
    26	  initialCelebId?: string | null;
    27	  /** 인물 열기/닫기 (호스트가 URL 동기화에 사용) */
    28	  onCelebChange?: (celebId: string | null) => void;
    29	  onComplete?: (result: CelebSearchResult) => void;
    30	  completeLabel?: string;
    31	  /** 저장한 인물/최근 찾은 인물 목록 표시 (기본 true) */
    32	  showLibrary?: boolean;
    33	  /** 사진으로 찾기 표시 (기본 true) */
    34	  allowPhoto?: boolean;
    35	  /** 처음에 바로 검색할 이름 (URL ?q= 등) */
    36	  initialQuery?: string | null;
    37	}
    38	
    39	type Busy = { kind: 'search' } | { kind: 'identify' } | { kind: 'confirm'; name: string } | null;
    40	
    41	const errMsg = (e: unknown) => (e instanceof CelebApiError || e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
    42	
    43	export function CelebSearchFlow({
    44	  apiBase = '',
    45	  selection = 'multiple',
    46	  initialCelebId = null,
    47	  onCelebChange,
    48	  onComplete,
    49	  completeLabel = '이 사진으로 계속',
    50	  showLibrary = true,
    51	  allowPhoto = true,
    52	  initialQuery = null,
    53	}: CelebSearchFlowProps) {
    54	  const api = useMemo(() => createCelebApi(apiBase), [apiBase]);
    55	
    56	  // 검색 단계
    57	  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<Busy>(null);
    58	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    59	  const [search, setSearch] = useState<SearchResponse | null>(null);
    60	  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | undefined>();
    61	  const [identify, setIdentify] = useState<IdentifyResponse | null>(null);
    62	  const [identifying, setIdentifying] = useState(false);
    63	  const [pendingPhotoCandidate, setPendingPhotoCandidate] = useState<CandidateItem | null>(null);
    64	  const [library, setLibrary] = useState<{ saved: CelebSummary[]; recent: CelebSummary[] }>({ saved: [], recent: [] });
    65	
    66	  // 인물 단계
    67	  const [celebId, setCelebId] = useState<string | null>(initialCelebId);
    68	  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<CelebDetailResponse | null>(null);
    69	  const [category, setCategory] = useState<CelebImageCategory>('signature');
    70	  const [collecting, setCollecting] = useState<Partial<Record<CelebImageCategory, boolean>>>({});
    71	  const [notices, setNotices] = useState<Partial<Record<CelebImageCategory, string[]>>>({});
    72	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    73	  const [uploadedPhoto, setUploadedPhoto] = useState<ImageRef | undefined>();
    74	  const attempted = useRef(new Set<string>());
    75	
    76	  const loadLibrary = useCallback(async () => {
    77	    if (!showLibrary) return;
    78	    try {
    79	      const [saved, recent] = await Promise.all([api.list({ saved: true, limit: 30 }), api.list({ saved: false, limit: 8 })]);
    80	      setLibrary({ saved: saved.items, recent: recent.items });
    81	    } catch {
    82	      /* 목록 실패는 검색을 막지 않는다 */
    83	    }
    84	  }, [api, showLibrary]);
    85	
    86	  useEffect(() => {
    87	    // 마운트 시 저장 목록을 불러온다 (외부 API와 동기화)
    88	    void loadLibrary();
    89	  }, [loadLibrary]);
    90	
    91	  useEffect(() => () => {
    92	    if (preview?.startsWith('blob:')) URL.revokeObjectURL(preview);
    93	  }, [preview]);
    94	
    95	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------ 인물 열기 */
    96	  const openCeleb = useCallback(
    97	    (id: string | null) => {
    98	      setCelebId(id);
    99	      setDetail(null);
   100	      setNotices({});
   101	      setCategory('signature');
   102	      setError(null);
   103	      onCelebChange?.(id);
   104	      if (!id) void loadLibrary();
   105	    },
   106	    [onCelebChange, loadLibrary],
   107	  );
   108	
   109	  useEffect(() => {
   110	    if (!celebId) return;
   111	    let alive = true;
   112	    api
   113	      .get(celebId)
   114	      .then((d) => alive && setDetail(d))
   115	      .catch((e) => {
   116	        if (!alive) return;
   117	        setError(errMsg(e));
   118	        setCelebId(null);
   119	        onCelebChange?.(null);
   120	      });
   121	    return () => {
   122	      alive = false;
   123	    };
   124	  }, [api, celebId, onCelebChange]);
   125	
   126	  const applyCollect = useCallback((r: CollectImagesResponse) => {
   127	    setDetail((d) => (d && d.celeb.id === r.celebId ? { ...d, images: { ...d.images, [r.category]: r.images } } : d));
   128	    setNotices((n) => ({ ...n, [r.category]: r.notices }));
   129	  }, []);
   130	
   131	  const collect = useCallback(
   132	    async (id: string, cat: CelebImageCategory, refresh = false) => {
   133	      setCollecting((c) => ({ ...c, [cat]: true }));
   134	      try {
   135	        applyCollect(await api.collect(id, cat, refresh));
   136	      } catch (e) {
   137	        setNotices((n) => ({ ...n, [cat]: [`사진을 모으지 못했습니다: ${errMsg(e)}`] }));
   138	      } finally {
   139	        setCollecting((c) => ({ ...c, [cat]: false }));
   140	      }
   141	    },
   142	    [api, applyCollect],
   143	  );
   144	
   145	  // 비어 있는 카테고리를 현재 탭부터 차례로 수집 (검색 결과는 DB에 캐시되어 다음에는 바로 열린다)
   146	  useEffect(() => {
   147	    if (!detail) return;
   148	    const id = detail.celeb.id;
   149	    const order = [category, ...CATEGORY_ORDER.filter((c) => c !== category)];
   150	    const todo = order.filter((c) => !detail.images[c].length && !attempted.current.has(`${id}:${c}`));
   151	    if (!todo.length || Object.values(collecting).some(Boolean)) return;
   152	    const next = todo[0];
   153	    attempted.current.add(`${id}:${next}`);
   154	    void collect(id, next);
   155	  }, [detail, category, collecting, collect]);
   156	
   157	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------ 검색 */
   158	  const runSearch = async (q: string) => {
   159	    setBusy({ kind: 'search' });
   160	    setError(null);
   161	    try {
   162	      setSearch(await api.search(q));
   163	    } catch (e) {
   164	      setError(errMsg(e));
   165	    } finally {
   166	      setBusy(null);
   167	    }
   168	  };
   169	
   170	  const runIdentify = async (file: File) => {
   171	    setError(null);
   172	    setIdentify(null);
   173	    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
   174	    setIdentifying(true);
   175	    try {
   176	      const rec = await api.uploadPhoto(file);
   177	      const r = await api.identify(rec.id);
   178	      setIdentify(r);
   179	    } catch (e) {
   180	      setError(`사진을 처리하지 못했습니다: ${errMsg(e)} (JPEG·PNG·WebP 사진을 올려 주세요)`);
   181	    } finally {
   182	      setIdentifying(false);
   183	    }
   184	  };
   185	
   186	  const confirm = async (c: CandidateItem, fromPhoto: boolean) => {
   187	    if (c.celebId) {
   188	      setUploadedPhoto(fromPhoto ? identify?.upload : undefined);
   189	      openCeleb(c.celebId);
   190	      return;
   191	    }
   192	    setBusy({ kind: 'confirm', name: c.name });
   193	    setError(null);
   194	    try {
   195	      const { celebId: _omit, saved: _saved, ...candidate } = c;
   196	      void _omit;
   197	      void _saved;
   198	      const { celeb } = await api.confirm(candidate);
   199	      setUploadedPhoto(fromPhoto ? identify?.upload : undefined);
   200	      openCeleb(celeb.id);
   201	    } catch (e) {
   202	      setError(errMsg(e));
   203	    } finally {
   204	      setBusy(null);
   205	    }
   206	  };
   207	
   208	  // 처음 검색어가 있으면 마운트 시 한 번 검색
   209	  const initialRun = useRef(false);
   210	  useEffect(() => {
   211	    if (initialRun.current || !initialQuery?.trim() || initialCelebId) return;
   212	    initialRun.current = true;
   213	    void runSearch(initialQuery.trim());
   214	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   215	  }, [initialQuery, initialCelebId]);
   216	
   217	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------ 선택 */
   218	  const allImages = useMemo(() => (detail ? CATEGORY_ORDER.flatMap((c) => detail.images[c]) : []), [detail]);
   219	  const selectedIds = allImages.filter((i) => i.selected).map((i) => i.id);
   220	
   221	  const patchLocal = (ids: string[], selected: boolean) =>
   222	    setDetail((d) => {
   223	      if (!d) return d;
   224	      const images = { ...d.images };
   225	      for (const c of CATEGORY_ORDER) images[c] = images[c].map((im) => (ids.includes(im.id) ? { ...im, selected } : im));
   226	      return { ...d, images };
   227	    });
   228	
   229	  const toggle = async (im: CelebImage) => {
   230	    const on = !im.selected;
   231	    const off = on && selection === 'single' ? selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== im.id) : [];
   232	    patchLocal([im.id], on);
   233	    if (off.length) patchLocal(off, false);
   234	    try {
   235	      await Promise.all([api.select(im.id, on), ...off.map((x) => api.select(x, false))]);
   236	    } catch (e) {
   237	      patchLocal([im.id], !on);
   238	      if (off.length) patchLocal(off, true);
   239	      setError(errMsg(e));
   240	    }
   241	  };
   242	
   243	  const toggleSave = async () => {
   244	    if (!detail) return;
   245	    setSaving(true);
   246	    try {
   247	      const { celeb } = await api.setSaved(detail.celeb.id, !detail.celeb.saved);
   248	      setDetail((d) => (d ? { ...d, celeb } : d));
   249	    } catch (e) {
   250	      setError(errMsg(e));
   251	    } finally {
   252	      setSaving(false);
   253	    }
   254	  };
   255	
   256	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------ 렌더 */
   257	  if (celebId) {
   258	    const selected = allImages.filter((i) => i.selected);
   259	    return (
   260	      <div className="cb-stack" style={{ gap: 'var(--cb-s5)' }}>
   261	        <div>
   262	          <button type="button" className={s.linkBtn} onClick={() => openCeleb(null)}>
   263	            다른 인물 찾기
   264	          </button>
   265	        </div>
   266	        {error ? <Notice tone="danger">{error}</Notice> : null}
   267	        {detail ? (
   268	          <>
   269	            <CelebProfileHeader celeb={detail.celeb} apiBase={apiBase} onToggleSave={toggleSave} saving={saving} />
   270	            <section>
   271	              <SectionHeader title="전신 룩" subtitle={selection === 'single' ? '분석할 사진 한 장을 고르세요.' : '참고할 사진을 고르세요. 여러 장 선택할 수 있습니다.'} />
   272	              <CelebGallery
   273	                images={detail.images}
   274	                category={category}
   275	                onCategoryChange={setCategory}
   276	                selectedIds={selectedIds}
   277	                onToggle={toggle}
   278	                apiBase={apiBase}
   279	                loading={collecting}
   280	                notices={notices}
   281	                onRefresh={(c) => void collect(detail.celeb.id, c, true)}
   282	              />
   283	            </section>
   284	            {selected.length ? (
   285	              <section>
   286	                <SectionHeader title="선택한 사진" subtitle={`${selected.length}장 · 대표/최근/평소 탭에 걸쳐 유지됩니다.`} />
   287	                <div className={s.selectedStrip}>
   288	                  {selected.map((im) => (
   289	                    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
   290	                    <img key={im.id} src={`${apiBase}${im.cached?.url ?? ''}`} alt="선택한 사진" />
   291	                  ))}
   292	                </div>
   293	              </section>
   294	            ) : null}
   295	            {onComplete ? (
   296	              <div className={s.footer}>
   297	                <span className={s.count}>{selected.length}장 선택</span>
   298	                <Button variant="primary" block disabled={!selected.length} onClick={() => onComplete({ celeb: detail.celeb, selectedImages: selected, uploadedPhoto })}>
   299	                  {completeLabel}
   300	                </Button>
   301	              </div>
   302	            ) : null}
   303	          </>
   304	        ) : (
   305	          <div className={s.skeleton} aria-busy="true" aria-label="불러오는 중">
   306	            {Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => (
   307	              <div key={i} />
   308	            ))}
   309	          </div>
   310	        )}
   311	      </div>
   312	    );
   313	  }
   314	
   315	  return (
   316	    <div className="cb-stack" style={{ gap: 'var(--cb-s5)' }}>
   317	      <CelebSearchBox
   318	        apiBase={apiBase}
   319	        initialQuery={initialQuery ?? ''}
   320	        busy={busy?.kind === 'search'}
   321	        onSearch={runSearch}
   322	        onPhoto={allowPhoto ? runIdentify : undefined}
   323	        onPickSuggestion={(it) => (it.celebId ? openCeleb(it.celebId) : void runSearch(it.name))}
   324	      />
   325	      {error ? <Notice tone="danger">{error}</Notice> : null}
   326	
   327	      {preview || identify ? (
   328	        <section>
   329	          <SectionHeader title="사진으로 찾기" right={identify ? <button type="button" className={s.linkBtn} onClick={() => { setIdentify(null); setPreview(undefined); }}>닫기</button> : undefined} />
   330	          <IdentifyPanel previewUrl={preview} result={identify} loading={identifying} apiBase={apiBase} />
   331	          {identify?.candidates.length ? (
   332	            <div style={{ marginTop: 'var(--cb-s4)' }}>
   333	              <div className="cb-row" style={{ justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
   334	                <span className="cb-row-title">{identify.needsNameSearch ? '데모 후보' : '사진 단서로 찾은 후보'}</span>
   335	                {identify.isMock ? <MockBadge /> : null}
   336	              </div>
   337	              <CandidateList items={identify.candidates} apiBase={apiBase} busyName={busy?.kind === 'confirm' ? busy.name : null} onSelect={(c) => setPendingPhotoCandidate(c)} selectLabel="확인" />
   338	            </div>
   339	          ) : null}
   340	        </section>
   341	      ) : null}
   342	
   343	      {search ? (
   344	        <section>
   345	          <SectionHeader
   346	            title="검색 결과"
   347	            subtitle={`"${search.query}" · 이름과 사진을 보고 맞는 인물을 고르세요.`}
   348	            right={search.isMock ? <MockBadge /> : undefined}
   349	          />
   350	          {search.notices.map((n) => (
   351	            <div key={n} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
   352	              <Notice>{n}</Notice>
   353	            </div>
   354	          ))}
   355	          <CandidateList items={search.candidates} apiBase={apiBase} busyName={busy?.kind === 'confirm' ? busy.name : null} onSelect={(c) => void confirm(c, false)} />
   356	        </section>
   357	      ) : null}
   358	
   359	      {showLibrary ? (
   360	        <>
   361	          <section>
   362	            <SectionHeader title="저장한 인물" />
   363	            <SavedCelebList
   364	              items={library.saved}
   365	              apiBase={apiBase}
   366	              onOpen={(id) => openCeleb(id)}
   367	              onDelete={async (id) => {
   368	                await api.remove(id);
   369	                await loadLibrary();
   370	              }}
   371	            />
   372	          </section>
   373	          {library.recent.length ? (
   374	            <section>
   375	              <SectionHeader title="최근 찾은 인물" subtitle="저장하지 않은 인물입니다." />
   376	              <SavedCelebList
   377	                items={library.recent}
   378	                apiBase={apiBase}
   379	                onOpen={(id) => openCeleb(id)}
   380	                onDelete={async (id) => {
   381	                  await api.remove(id);
   382	                  await loadLibrary();
   383	                }}
   384	              />
   385	            </section>
   386	          ) : null}
   387	        </>
   388	      ) : null}
   389	
   390	      <ConfirmDialog
   391	        open={!!pendingPhotoCandidate}
   392	        title={`${pendingPhotoCandidate?.name ?? ''}(으)로 확정할까요?`}
   393	        description="이 후보는 검색 결과 제목이나 사진 속 글자에서 나온 이름입니다. 사진 속 인물과 같은 사람인지는 확인하지 않았습니다."
   394	        items={pendingPhotoCandidate?.evidence.slice(0, 3)}
   395	        confirmLabel="이 인물로 확정"
   396	        cancelLabel="다시 고르기"
   397	        loading={busy?.kind === 'confirm'}
   398	        onCancel={() => setPendingPhotoCandidate(null)}
   399	        onConfirm={async () => {
   400	          const c = pendingPhotoCandidate;
   401	          if (!c) return;
   402	          await confirm(c, true);
   403	          setPendingPhotoCandidate(null);
   404	        }}
   405	      />
   406	    </div>
   407	  );
   408	}

     1	// 호스트 앱이 재노출할 Route Handler 함수들. "@celeber/search-celeb/routes"
     2	// 경로 네임스페이스: /api/celeb/*  (README의 재노출 표 참고)
     3	//   app/api/celeb/suggest/route.ts           export { suggestGET as GET }
     4	//   app/api/celeb/search/route.ts            export { searchGET as GET }
     5	//   app/api/celeb/identify/route.ts          export { identifyPOST as POST }
     6	//   app/api/celeb/celebs/route.ts            export { celebsGET as GET, celebsPOST as POST }
     7	//   app/api/celeb/celebs/[id]/route.ts       export { celebGET as GET, celebPATCH as PATCH, celebDELETE as DELETE }
     8	//   app/api/celeb/celebs/[id]/images/route.ts export { celebImagesGET as GET, celebImagesPOST as POST }

    12	import { z } from 'zod';

    15	import { deleteCeleb, getCelebDetail, listCelebSummaries, setCelebSaved, setImageSelected, confirmCandidate } from '../server/celebs';
    16	import { identifyFromPhoto } from '../server/identify';

    20	type IdCtx = { params: Promise<{ id: string }> };

    22	const qParam = (req: Request) => (new URL(req.url).searchParams.get('q') ?? '').trim();
    23	
    24	/** GET /api/celeb/suggest?q= — 자동완성 (DB + 데모 인물) */
    25	export const suggestGET = withErrors(async (req: Request) => Response.json(suggestCelebs(qParam(req))));
    26	
    27	/** GET /api/celeb/search?q= — 이름 검색 후보 (썸네일 포함) */
    28	export const searchGET = withErrors(async (req: Request) => {
    29	  const q = qParam(req);
    30	  if (!q) return jsonError(400, 'empty_query', '검색어를 입력해 주세요.');
    31	  if (q.length > MAX_QUERY_LENGTH) return jsonError(400, 'query_too_long', `검색어는 ${MAX_QUERY_LENGTH}자 이내로 입력해 주세요.`);
    32	  return Response.json(await searchCelebCandidates(q));
    33	});
    34	
    35	/** POST /api/celeb/identify { uploadId } — 사진 단서로 후보 (얼굴 인식 없음) */
    36	export const identifyPOST = withErrors(async (req: Request) => {
    37	  const body = await parseJsonBody(req, z.object({ uploadId: z.string().min(1) }));
    38	  if (!body.ok) return body.response;
    39	  const r = await identifyFromPhoto(body.data.uploadId);
    40	  return r ? Response.json(r) : jsonError(404, 'not_found', '업로드한 사진을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    41	});
    42	
    43	/** GET /api/celeb/celebs?saved=1&limit= — 인물 목록 (저장/최근) */
    44	export const celebsGET = withErrors(async (req: Request) => {
    45	  const sp = new URL(req.url).searchParams;
    46	  const saved = sp.get('saved');
    47	  const limit = Math.max(1, Math.min(100, Number(sp.get('limit')) || 30));
    48	  return Response.json({ items: listCelebSummaries({ saved: saved === null ? undefined : saved === '1' || saved === 'true', limit }) });
    49	});

    51	/** POST /api/celeb/celebs { candidate } — 사용자가 확인한 후보를 인물로 확정 */
    52	export const celebsPOST = withErrors(async (req: Request) => {
    53	  const body = await parseJsonBody(req, z.object({ candidate: CelebCandidateSchema }));
    54	  if (!body.ok) return body.response;
    55	  if (!body.data.candidate.name.trim()) return jsonError(400, 'empty_name', '이름이 비어 있습니다.');
    56	  return Response.json({ celeb: await confirmCandidate(body.data.candidate) });
    57	});

    59	/** GET /api/celeb/celebs/[id] — 인물 + 카테고리별 사진 */
    60	export const celebGET = withErrors(async (_req: Request, ctx: IdCtx) => {
    61	  const { id } = await ctx.params;
    62	  const d = getCelebDetail(id);
    63	  return d ? Response.json(d) : jsonError(404, 'not_found', '인물을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    64	});
    65	
    66	/** PATCH /api/celeb/celebs/[id] { saved } — 저장 토글 */
    67	export const celebPATCH = withErrors(async (req: Request, ctx: IdCtx) => {
    68	  const { id } = await ctx.params;
    69	  const body = await parseJsonBody(req, z.object({ saved: z.boolean() }));
    70	  if (!body.ok) return body.response;
    71	  const c = setCelebSaved(id, body.data.saved);
    72	  return c ? Response.json({ celeb: c }) : jsonError(404, 'not_found', '인물을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    73	});
    74	
    75	/** DELETE /api/celeb/celebs/[id] — 인물과 수집 사진 기록 삭제 (UI는 ConfirmDialog를 거친다) */
    76	export const celebDELETE = withErrors(async (_req: Request, ctx: IdCtx) => {
    77	  const { id } = await ctx.params;
    78	  const r = deleteCeleb(id);
    79	  return r.deleted ? Response.json(r) : jsonError(404, 'not_found', '인물을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    80	});
    81

    84	  const { id } = await ctx.params;

    86	  return d ? Response.json({ celebId: id, images: d.images }) : jsonError(404, 'not_found', '인물을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    87	});

    89	/** POST /api/celeb/celebs/[id]/images { category, refresh? } — 검색·캐시·판정 후 저장 */
    90	export const celebImagesPOST = withErrors(async (req: Request, ctx: IdCtx) => {
    91	  const { id } = await ctx.params;
    92	  const body = await parseJsonBody(req, z.object({ category: CelebImageCategorySchema, refresh: z.boolean().optional() }));
    93	  if (!body.ok) return body.response;
    94	  const r = await collectCelebImages(id, body.data.category, { refresh: body.data.refresh });
    95	  return r ? Response.json(r) : jsonError(404, 'not_found', '인물을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    96	});
    97	
    98	/** PATCH /api/celeb/images/[id] { selected } — 사진 선택 표시 */
    99	export const imagePATCH = withErrors(async (req: Request, ctx: IdCtx) => {
   100	  const { id } = await ctx.params;
   101	  const body = await parseJsonBody(req, z.object({ selected: z.boolean() }));
   102	  if (!body.ok) return body.response;
   103	  const im = setImageSelected(id, body.data.selected);
   104	  return im ? Response.json({ image: im }) : jsonError(404, 'not_found', '사진을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
   105	});

     1	'use client';
     2	/**
     3	 * 연예인 검색 플로우 (재사용): 이름/사진으로 후보 찾기 → 사용자가 확정 → 대표·최근·평소 전신 사진 선택.
     4	 * 완료 시 onComplete({ celeb, selectedImages, uploadedPhoto }).
     5	 * API는 apiBase + '/api/celeb/*', '/api/uploads'로 호출한다 (호스트 앱이 같은 경로를 재노출해야 함).
     6	 */
     7	import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
     8	import type { CelebImage, CelebImageCategory, ImageRef } from '@celeber/core/contracts';
     9	import { Button, ConfirmDialog, MockBadge, Notice, SectionHeader } from '@celeber/ui';
    10	import { CelebApiError, createCelebApi } from '../lib/api';
    11	import { CATEGORY_ORDER } from '../lib/queries';
    12	import type { CandidateItem, CelebDetailResponse, CelebSearchResult, CelebSummary, CollectImagesResponse, IdentifyResponse, SearchResponse } from '../lib/types';
    13	import { CandidateList } from '../components/CandidateList';
    14	import { CelebGallery } from '../components/CelebGallery';
    15	import { CelebProfileHeader } from '../components/CelebProfileHeader';
    16	import { CelebSearchBox } from '../components/CelebSearchBox';
    17	import { IdentifyPanel } from '../components/IdentifyPanel';
    18	import { SavedCelebList } from '../components/SavedCelebList';
    19	import s from '../components/search-celeb.module.css';
    20	
    21	export interface CelebSearchFlowProps {
    22	  apiBase?: string;
    23	  /** 사진 선택 방식 (기본 multiple). celeb-vibe-report처럼 한 장만 필요하면 'single' */
    24	  selection?: 'multiple' | 'single';
    25	  /** 처음부터 열 인물 (URL ?celeb= 등) */
    26	  initialCelebId?: string | null;
    27	  /** 인물 열기/닫기 (호스트가 URL 동기화에 사용) */
    28	  onCelebChange?: (celebId: string | null) => void;
    29	  onComplete?: (result: CelebSearchResult) => void;
    30	  completeLabel?: string;
    31	  /** 저장한 인물/최근 찾은 인물 목록 표시 (기본 true) */
    32	  showLibrary?: boolean;
    33	  /** 사진으로 찾기 표시 (기본 true) */
    34	  allowPhoto?: boolean;
    35	  /** 처음에 바로 검색할 이름 (URL ?q= 등) */
    36	  initialQuery?: string | null;
    37	}
    38	
    39	type Busy = { kind: 'search' } | { kind: 'identify' } | { kind: 'confirm'; name: string } | null;
    40	
    41	const errMsg = (e: unknown) => (e instanceof CelebApiError || e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
    42	
    43	export function CelebSearchFlow({
    44	  apiBase = '',
    45	  selection = 'multiple',
    46	  initialCelebId = null,
    47	  onCelebChange,
    48	  onComplete,
    49	  completeLabel = '이 사진으로 계속',
    50	  showLibrary = true,
    51	  allowPhoto = true,
    52	  initialQuery = null,
    53	}: CelebSearchFlowProps) {
    54	  const api = useMemo(() => createCelebApi(apiBase), [apiBase]);
    55	
    56	  // 검색 단계
    57	  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<Busy>(null);
    58	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    59	  const [search, setSearch] = useState<SearchResponse | null>(null);
    60	  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | undefined>();
    61	  const [identify, setIdentify] = useState<IdentifyResponse | null>(null);
    62	  const [identifying, setIdentifying] = useState(false);
    63	  const [pendingPhotoCandidate, setPendingPhotoCandidate] = useState<CandidateItem | null>(null);
    64	  const [library, setLibrary] = useState<{ saved: CelebSummary[]; recent: CelebSummary[] }>({ saved: [], recent: [] });
    65	
    66	  // 인물 단계
    67	  const [celebId, setCelebId] = useState<string | null>(initialCelebId);
    68	  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<CelebDetailResponse | null>(null);
    69	  const [category, setCategory] = useState<CelebImageCategory>('signature');
    70	  const [collecting, setCollecting] = useState<Partial<Record<CelebImageCategory, boolean>>>({});
    71	  const [notices, setNotices] = useState<Partial<Record<CelebImageCategory, string[]>>>({});
    72	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    73	  const [uploadedPhoto, setUploadedPhoto] = useState<ImageRef | undefined>();
    74	  const attempted = useRef(new Set<string>());
    75	
    76	  const loadLibrary = useCallback(async () => {
    77	    if (!showLibrary) return;
    78	    try {
    79	      const [saved, recent] = await Promise.all([api.list({ saved: true, limit: 30 }), api.list({ saved: false, limit: 8 })]);
    80	      setLibrary({ saved: saved.items, recent: recent.items });
    81	    } catch {
    82	      /* 목록 실패는 검색을 막지 않는다 */
    83	    }
    84	  }, [api, showLibrary]);
    85	
    86	  useEffect(() => {
    87	    // 마운트 시 저장 목록을 불러온다 (외부 API와 동기화)
    88	    void loadLibrary();
    89	  }, [loadLibrary]);
    90	
    91	  useEffect(() => () => {
    92	    if (preview?.startsWith('blob:')) URL.revokeObjectURL(preview);
    93	  }, [preview]);
    94	
    95	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------ 인물 열기 */
    96	  const openCeleb = useCallback(
    97	    (id: string | null) => {
    98	      setCelebId(id);
    99	      setDetail(null);
   100	      setNotices({});
   101	      setCategory('signature');
   102	      setError(null);
   103	      onCelebChange?.(id);
   104	      if (!id) void loadLibrary();
   105	    },
   106	    [onCelebChange, loadLibrary],
   107	  );
   108	
   109	  useEffect(() => {
   110	    if (!celebId) return;
   111	    let alive = true;
   112	    api
   113	      .get(celebId)
   114	      .then((d) => alive && setDetail(d))
   115	      .catch((e) => {
   116	        if (!alive) return;
   117	        setError(errMsg(e));
   118	        setCelebId(null);
   119	        onCelebChange?.(null);
   120	      });
   121	    return () => {
   122	      alive = false;
   123	    };
   124	  }, [api, celebId, onCelebChange]);
   125	
   126	  const applyCollect = useCallback((r: CollectImagesResponse) => {
   127	    setDetail((d) => (d && d.celeb.id === r.celebId ? { ...d, images: { ...d.images, [r.category]: r.images } } : d));
   128	    setNotices((n) => ({ ...n, [r.category]: r.notices }));
   129	  }, []);
   130	
   131	  const collect = useCallback(
   132	    async (id: string, cat: CelebImageCategory, refresh = false) => {
   133	      setCollecting((c) => ({ ...c, [cat]: true }));
   134	      try {
   135	        applyCollect(await api.collect(id, cat, refresh));
   136	      } catch (e) {
   137	        setNotices((n) => ({ ...n, [cat]: [`사진을 모으지 못했습니다: ${errMsg(e)}`] }));
   138	      } finally {
   139	        setCollecting((c) => ({ ...c, [cat]: false }));
   140	      }
   141	    },
   142	    [api, applyCollect],
   143	  );
   144	
   145	  // 비어 있는 카테고리를 현재 탭부터 차례로 수집 (검색 결과는 DB에 캐시되어 다음에는 바로 열린다)
   146	  useEffect(() => {
   147	    if (!detail) return;
   148	    const id = detail.celeb.id;
   149	    const order = [category, ...CATEGORY_ORDER.filter((c) => c !== category)];
   150	    const todo = order.filter((c) => !detail.images[c].length && !attempted.current.has(`${id}:${c}`));
   151	    if (!todo.length || Object.values(collecting).some(Boolean)) return;
   152	    const next = todo[0];
   153	    attempted.current.add(`${id}:${next}`);
   154	    void collect(id, next);
   155	  }, [detail, category, collecting, collect]);
   156	
   157	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------ 검색 */
   158	  const runSearch = async (q: string) => {
   159	    setBusy({ kind: 'search' });
   160	    setError(null);
   161	    try {
   162	      setSearch(await api.search(q));
   163	    } catch (e) {
   164	      setError(errMsg(e));
   165	    } finally {
   166	      setBusy(null);
   167	    }
   168	  };
   169	
   170	  const runIdentify = async (file: File) => {
   171	    setError(null);
   172	    setIdentify(null);
   173	    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(file));
   174	    setIdentifying(true);
   175	    try {
   176	      const rec = await api.uploadPhoto(file);
   177	      const r = await api.identify(rec.id);
   178	      setIdentify(r);
   179	    } catch (e) {
   180	      setError(`사진을 처리하지 못했습니다: ${errMsg(e)} (JPEG·PNG·WebP 사진을 올려 주세요)`);
   181	    } finally {
   182	      setIdentifying(false);
   183	    }
   184	  };
   185	
   186	  const confirm = async (c: CandidateItem, fromPhoto: boolean) => {
   187	    if (c.celebId) {
   188	      setUploadedPhoto(fromPhoto ? identify?.upload : undefined);
   189	      openCeleb(c.celebId);
   190	      return;
   191	    }
   192	    setBusy({ kind: 'confirm', name: c.name });
   193	    setError(null);
   194	    try {
   195	      const { celebId: _omit, saved: _saved, ...candidate } = c;
   196	      void _omit;
   197	      void _saved;
   198	      const { celeb } = await api.confirm(candidate);
   199	      setUploadedPhoto(fromPhoto ? identify?.upload : undefined);
   200	      openCeleb(celeb.id);
   201	    } catch (e) {
   202	      setError(errMsg(e));
   203	    } finally {
   204	      setBusy(null);
   205	    }
   206	  };
   207	
   208	  // 처음 검색어가 있으면 마운트 시 한 번 검색
   209	  const initialRun = useRef(false);
   210	  useEffect(() => {
   211	    if (initialRun.current || !initialQuery?.trim() || initialCelebId) return;
   212	    initialRun.current = true;
   213	    void runSearch(initialQuery.trim());
   214	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   215	  }, [initialQuery, initialCelebId]);
   216	
   217	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------ 선택 */
   218	  const allImages = useMemo(() => (detail ? CATEGORY_ORDER.flatMap((c) => detail.images[c]) : []), [detail]);
   219	  const selectedIds = allImages.filter((i) => i.selected).map((i) => i.id);
   220	
   221	  const patchLocal = (ids: string[], selected: boolean) =>
   222	    setDetail((d) => {
   223	      if (!d) return d;
   224	      const images = { ...d.images };
   225	      for (const c of CATEGORY_ORDER) images[c] = images[c].map((im) => (ids.includes(im.id) ? { ...im, selected } : im));
   226	      return { ...d, images };
   227	    });
   228	
   229	  const toggle = async (im: CelebImage) => {
   230	    const on = !im.selected;
   231	    const off = on && selection === 'single' ? selectedIds.filter((x) => x !== im.id) : [];
   232	    patchLocal([im.id], on);
   233	    if (off.length) patchLocal(off, false);
   234	    try {
   235	      await Promise.all([api.select(im.id, on), ...off.map((x) => api.select(x, false))]);
   236	    } catch (e) {
   237	      patchLocal([im.id], !on);
   238	      if (off.length) patchLocal(off, true);
   239	      setError(errMsg(e));
   240	    }
   241	  };
   242	
   243	  const toggleSave = async () => {
   244	    if (!detail) return;
   245	    setSaving(true);
   246	    try {
   247	      const { celeb } = await api.setSaved(detail.celeb.id, !detail.celeb.saved);
   248	      setDetail((d) => (d ? { ...d, celeb } : d));
   249	    } catch (e) {
   250	      setError(errMsg(e));
   251	    } finally {
   252	      setSaving(false);
   253	    }
   254	  };
   255	
   256	  /* ------------------------------------------------------------ 렌더 */
   257	  if (celebId) {
   258	    const selected = allImages.filter((i) => i.selected);
   259	    return (
   260	      <div className="cb-stack" style={{ gap: 'var(--cb-s5)' }}>
   261	        <div>
   262	          <button type="button" className={s.linkBtn} onClick={() => openCeleb(null)}>
   263	            다른 인물 찾기
   264	          </button>
   265	        </div>
   266	        {error ? <Notice tone="danger">{error}</Notice> : null}
   267	        {detail ? (
   268	          <>
   269	            <CelebProfileHeader celeb={detail.celeb} apiBase={apiBase} onToggleSave={toggleSave} saving={saving} />
   270	            <section>
   271	              <SectionHeader title="전신 룩" subtitle={selection === 'single' ? '분석할 사진 한 장을 고르세요.' : '참고할 사진을 고르세요. 여러 장 선택할 수 있습니다.'} />
   272	              <CelebGallery
   273	                images={detail.images}
   274	                category={category}
   275	                onCategoryChange={setCategory}
   276	                selectedIds={selectedIds}
   277	                onToggle={toggle}
   278	                apiBase={apiBase}
   279	                loading={collecting}
   280	                notices={notices}
   281	                onRefresh={(c) => void collect(detail.celeb.id, c, true)}
   282	              />
   283	            </section>
   284	            {selected.length ? (
   285	              <section>
   286	                <SectionHeader title="선택한 사진" subtitle={`${selected.length}장 · 대표/최근/평소 탭에 걸쳐 유지됩니다.`} />
   287	                <div className={s.selectedStrip}>
   288	                  {selected.map((im) => (
   289	                    // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
   290	                    <img key={im.id} src={`${apiBase}${im.cached?.url ?? ''}`} alt="선택한 사진" />
   291	                  ))}
   292	                </div>
   293	              </section>
   294	            ) : null}
   295	            {onComplete ? (
   296	              <div className={s.footer}>
   297	                <span className={s.count}>{selected.length}장 선택</span>
   298	                <Button variant="primary" block disabled={!selected.length} onClick={() => onComplete({ celeb: detail.celeb, selectedImages: selected, uploadedPhoto })}>
   299	                  {completeLabel}
   300	                </Button>
   301	              </div>
   302	            ) : null}
   303	          </>
   304	        ) : (
   305	          <div className={s.skeleton} aria-busy="true" aria-label="불러오는 중">
   306	            {Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => (
   307	              <div key={i} />
   308	            ))}
   309	          </div>
   310	        )}
   311	      </div>
   312	    );
   313	  }
   314	
   315	  return (
   316	    <div className="cb-stack" style={{ gap: 'var(--cb-s5)' }}>
   317	      <CelebSearchBox
   318	        apiBase={apiBase}
   319	        initialQuery={initialQuery ?? ''}
   320	        busy={busy?.kind === 'search'}
   321	        onSearch={runSearch}
   322	        onPhoto={allowPhoto ? runIdentify : undefined}
   323	        onPickSuggestion={(it) => (it.celebId ? openCeleb(it.celebId) : void runSearch(it.name))}
   324	      />
   325	      {error ? <Notice tone="danger">{error}</Notice> : null}
   326	
   327	      {preview || identify ? (
   328	        <section>
   329	          <SectionHeader title="사진으로 찾기" right={identify ? <button type="button" className={s.linkBtn} onClick={() => { setIdentify(null); setPreview(undefined); }}>닫기</button> : undefined} />
   330	          <IdentifyPanel previewUrl={preview} result={identify} loading={identifying} apiBase={apiBase} />
   331	          {identify?.candidates.length ? (
   332	            <div style={{ marginTop: 'var(--cb-s4)' }}>
   333	              <div className="cb-row" style={{ justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
   334	                <span className="cb-row-title">{identify.needsNameSearch ? '데모 후보' : '사진 단서로 찾은 후보'}</span>
   335	                {identify.isMock ? <MockBadge /> : null}
   336	              </div>
   337	              <CandidateList items={identify.candidates} apiBase={apiBase} busyName={busy?.kind === 'confirm' ? busy.name : null} onSelect={(c) => setPendingPhotoCandidate(c)} selectLabel="확인" />
   338	            </div>

   340	        </section>

   348	            right={search.isMock ? <MockBadge /> : undefined}

   382	                  await loadLibrary();

   384	              />

   386	          ) : null}
   387	        </>
   388	      ) : null}
   389	
   390	      <ConfirmDialog
   391	        open={!!pendingPhotoCandidate}
   392	        title={`${pendingPhotoCandidate?.name ?? ''}(으)로 확정할까요?`}
   393	        description="이 후보는 검색 결과 제목이나 사진 속 글자에서 나온 이름입니다. 사진 속 인물과 같은 사람인지는 확인하지 않았습니다."
   394	        items={pendingPhotoCandidate?.evidence.slice(0, 3)}
   395	        confirmLabel="이 인물로 확정"
   396	        cancelLabel="다시 고르기"
   397	        loading={busy?.kind === 'confirm'}
   398	        onCancel={() => setPendingPhotoCandidate(null)}
   399	        onConfirm={async () => {
   400	          const c = pendingPhotoCandidate;
   401	          if (!c) return;
   402	          await confirm(c, true);
   403	          setPendingPhotoCandidate(null);
   404	        }}
   405	      />
   406	    </div>
   407	  );

     1	/**
     2	 * 업로드/캐시 이미지 저장과 서빙. 파일: <dataDir>/uploads/<id>.<ext>, 메타: uploads 테이블.
     3	 * - 래스터 이미지는 sharp로 EXIF 회전 보정 + sRGB 변환(Display P3 등 → sRGB) + 최대 변 제한
     4	 * - sha256 + kind 동일하면 기존 레코드 재사용 (중복 제거)
     5	 */
     6	import 'server-only';
     7	import crypto from 'node:crypto';
     8	import fs from 'node:fs';
     9	import path from 'node:path';
    10	import sharp from 'sharp';
    11	import type { ImageRef, UploadKind, UploadRecord } from '../contracts';
    12	import { UploadKindSchema } from '../contracts';
    13	import { dataDir, getDb, newId, nowIso } from './db';
    14	import { jsonError } from './http';
    15	
    16	export const MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024;
    17	/** 저장 시 최대 변 길이(px). 분석 정밀도와 용량의 절충 */
    18	export const MAX_STORED_EDGE = 2560;
    19	
    20	const ID_RE = /^up_[a-z0-9]+$/;
    21	const EXT: Record<string, string> = {
    22	  'image/jpeg': 'jpg',
    23	  'image/png': 'png',
    24	  'image/webp': 'webp',
    25	  'image/gif': 'gif',
    26	  'image/svg+xml': 'svg',
    27	  'image/heic': 'heic',
    28	  'image/avif': 'avif',
    29	};

    31	export function uploadsDir(): string {
    32	  const d = path.join(dataDir(), 'uploads');
    33	  fs.mkdirSync(d, { recursive: true });
    34	  return d;
    35	}
    36	export const uploadUrl = (id: string): string => `/api/uploads/${id}`;
